\u5c1d\u8bd5\u89e3\u6790\u65b0\u683c\u5f0f \"score|gbest_f|initial_gbest_f|opt_val\"\n    38\t        parts = str(pred).split('|')\n    39\t        if len(parts) == 4:\n    40\t            score = float(parts[0])\n    41\t            gbest_f = float(parts[1])\n    42\t            scores.append(score)\n    43\t            gbest_f_vals.append(gbest_f)\n    44\t\n    45\t    total = len(df[df['split'] == 'test']) if 'split' in df.columns else len(df)\n    46\t    if len(scores) == 0:\n    47\t        report = {\"score\": 0.0, \"mean_gbest_f\": None, \"total\": total}\n    48\t    else:\n    49\t        mean_score = float(sum(scores) / len(scores))\n    50\t        mean_gbest = float(sum(gbest_f_vals) / len(gbest_f_vals)) if gbest_f_vals else None\n    51\t        report = {\"score\": float(mean_score), \"mean_gbest_f\": float(mean_gbest) if mean_gbest is not None else None, \"total\": total, \"mean_score\": float(mean_score)}\n    52\t\n    53\t    report_path = os.path.join(dname, \"report.json\")\n    54\t    with open(report_path, \"w\") as f:\n    55\t        json.dump(report, f, indent=2)\n    56\t\n    57\t    print(json.dumps(report, indent=2))\n    58\t    return report, report_path\n    59\t\n    60\t\n    61\tif __name__ == '__main__':\n    62\t    import argparse\n    63\t\n    64\t    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    65\t    parser.add_argument('--dname', required=True)\n    66\t    args = parser.parse_args()\n    67\t    report_bbob_unconstrained(args.dname)\n\"\n    }\n    </json>"

Train every update_freq steps\n   190\t        if self.step_count % self.update_freq == 0 and len(self.replay_buffer) >= self.batch_size:\n   191\t            loss = self._train_step()\n   192\t            self.total_loss += loss\n   193\t            self.loss_history.append(loss)\n   194\t\n   195\t    def _train_step(self) -> float:\n   196\t        \"\"\"Perform one DQN training step on a batch from the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n   197\t        batch = self.rng.choice(len(self.replay_buffer), self.batch_size, replace=False).tolist()\n   198\t        batch = [self.replay_buffer[i] for i in batch]\n   199\t\n   200\t        obs_b = torch.stack([t[0] for t in batch])\n   201\t        act_b = torch.tensor([t[1] for t in batch], dtype=torch.long, device=self.device)\n   202\t        rew_b = torch.tensor([t[2] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   203\t        next_b = torch.stack([t[3] for t in batch])\n   204\t        done_b = torch.tensor([t[4] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   205\t\n   206\t        q_values = self.net(obs_b)\n   207\t        q_sa = q_values.gather(1, act_b.unsqueeze(1)).squeeze(1)\n   208\t\n   209\t        with torch.no_grad():\n   210\t            q_next = self.target_net(next_b)\n   211\t            q_next_max = q_next.max(1)[0]\n   212\t            target = rew_b + self.gamma * (1.0 - done_b) * q_next_max\n   213\t\n   214\t        loss = F.mse_loss(q_sa, target)\n   215\t        self.optimizer.zero_grad()\n   216\t        loss.backward()\n   217\t        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(self.net.parameters(), self.max_grad_norm)\n   218\t        self.optimizer.step()\n   219\t\n   220\t        # Update target network periodically\n   221\t        if self.step_count % self.target_update_freq == 0:\n   222\t            self.target_net.load_state_dict(self.net.state_dict())\n   223\t            # Decay epsilon\n   224\t            self.epsilon = max(self.epsilon_end, self.epsilon * self.epsilon_decay)\n   225\t\n   226\t        return loss.item()\n   227\t\n   228\t    def reset_loss(self):\n   229\t        \"\"\"Reset loss and reward counters for a new sweep.\"\"\"\n   230\t        self.total_loss = 0.0\n   231\t        self.total_reward = 0.0\n   232\t        self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   233\t        self.reward_history = []\n   234\t\n   235\t    def get_loss_history(self) -> list:\n   236\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded losses.\"\"\"\n   237\t        return self.loss_history\n   238\t\n   239\t    def get_reward_history(self) -> list:\n   240\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded returns (every N updates).\"\"\"\n   241\t        return self.reward_history\n   242\t\n   243\t    def get_total_loss(self) -> float:\n   244\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative training loss.\"\"\"\n   245\t        return self.total_loss\n   246\t\n   247\t    def get_total_reward(self) -> float:\n   248\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative reward from environment interactions.\"\"\"\n   249\t        return self.total_reward\n   250\t\n   251\t    def action_to_index(self, action: Dict[str, float]) -> int:\n   252\t        a = np.array([action.get('F', 0.8), action.get('CR', 0.9)], dtype=float)\n   253\t        acts = np.array([[aa['F'], aa['CR']] for aa in self.actions], dtype=float)\n   254\t        d = np.sum((acts - a[None, :])**2, axis=1)\n   255\t        return int(np.argmin(d))\n   256\t\n   257\t    def save(self, path: str):\n   258\t        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n   259\t\n   260\t    def load(self, path: str):\n   261\t        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n\"\n    }\n    </json>",

\u5982\u679c\u63d0\u4f9b\u4e86\u79cd\u5b50\uff0c\u4f7f\u7528\u5b83\u6765\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u6743\u91cd\n    34\t            if seed is not None:\n    35\t                self._init_weights(seed)\n    36\t\n    37\t        def _init_weights(self, seed):\n    38\t            \"\"\"\u4f7f\u7528\u56fa\u5b9a\u79cd\u5b50\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u7f51\u7edc\u6743\u91cd\"\"\"\n    39\t            g = torch.Generator()\n    40\t            g.manual_seed(seed)\n    41\t            \n    42\t            # \u4f7f\u7528Kaiming\u521d\u59cb\u5316\n    43\t            for m in [self.fc1, self.fc2, self.fc3, self.out]:\n    44\t                torch.nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(m.weight, nonlinearity='leaky_relu')\n    45\t                torch.nn.init.zeros_(m.bias)\n    46\t\n    47\t        def forward(self, x):\n    48\t            x = x.type_as(self.fc1.weight)\n    49\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc1(x), 0.2)\n    50\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc2(x), 0.2)\n    51\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc3(x), 0.2)\n    52\t            return self.out(x)\n    53\t\n    54\t\n    55\tclass DQNController(ParamController):\n    56\t    \"\"\"Simple continuous-action DQN controller for DE parameters.\n    57\t\n    58\t    The network outputs unbounded real values; `step` maps them to\n    59\t    DE parameter ranges: F in [0.1, 1.0], CR in [0.0, 1.0].\n    60\t    \"\"\"\n    61\t    def __init__(self, model_path: str = None, obs_dim: int = 3, device: str = 'cpu'):\n    62\t        if torch is None:\n    63\t            raise RuntimeError('torch is required for DQNController')\n    64\t        self.device = torch.device(device)\n    65\t        self.obs_dim = obs_dim\n    66\t        self.action_dim = 2  # F, CR\n    67\t        self.net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.action_dim).to(self.device)\n    68\t        if model_path and os.path.exists(model_path):\n    69\t            self.load(model_path)\n    70\t\n    71\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    72\t        return None\n    73\t\n    74\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    75\t        it = float(observation.get('iteration', 0))\n    76\t        evals = float(observation.get('evals', 0))\n    77\t        gbest_f = float(observation.get('gbest_f', 0.0))\n    78\t        init_f = float(observation.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f))\n    79\t        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', max(1.0, evals)))\n    80\t        # normalized progress and relative improvement\n    81\t        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\n    82\t        if init_f != 0.0 and gbest_f != 0.0 and np.isfinite(init_f) and np.isfinite(gbest_f):\n    83\t            rel_improve = max(0.0, min(1.0, (np.log(abs(init_f)) - np.log(abs(gbest_f))) / max(1.0, abs(np.log(abs(init_f))))))\n    84\t        else:\n    85\t            rel_improve = 0.0\n    86\t        obs = [progress, rel_improve, gbest_f]\n    87\t        \n    88\t        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n    89\t        x = torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device).unsqueeze(0)\n    90\t        \n    91\t        with torch.no_grad():\n    92\t            out_t = self.net(x).squeeze(0).cpu()\n    93\t            try:\n    94\t                out_t = torch.nan_to_num(out_t, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n    95\t            except Exception:\n    96\t                out_np = out_t.numpy()\n    97\t                out_np = np.nan_to_num(out_np, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n    98\t                out_t = torch.from_numpy(out_np)\n    99\t            out_t = torch.clamp(out_t, -10.0, 10.0)\n   100\t\n   101\t        out = out_t.numpy()\n   102\t\n   103\t        # Map F (differential weight) with sigmoid to [0.1, 1.0]\n   104\t        try:\n   105\t            f_raw = float(out[0])\n   106\t            f_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-f_raw)))\n   107\t        except Exception:\n   108\t            f_sig = 0.5\n   109\t        F = 0.1 + (1.0 - 0.1) * f_sig\n   110\t        \n   111\t        # Map CR (crossover probability) with sigmoid to [0.0, 1.0]\n   112\t        try:\n   113\t            cr_raw = float(out[1])\n   114\t            cr_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-cr_raw)))\n   115\t        except Exception:\n   116\t            cr_sig = 0.9\n   117\t        CR = 0.0 + (1.0 - 0.0) * cr_sig\n   118\t        \n   119\t        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR)}\n   120\t\n   121\t    def save(self, path: str):\n   122\t        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n   123\t\n   124\t    def load(self, path: str):\n   125\t        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n   126\t\n   127\t\n   128\tclass DiscreteDQNController(ParamController):\n   129\t    \"\"\"Discrete-action DQN controller for DE parameters with online training.\n   130\t\n   131\t    Discretizes F and CR into `bins` equally spaced values and\n   132\t    treats the Cartesian product as the discrete action space.\n   133\t    The network outpu\n   134\t<response clipped>\n   135\trm]\n   136\t        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n   137\t        return torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device)\n   138\t\n   139\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n   140\t        x = self._obs_to_tensor(observation)\n   141\t\n   142\t        # Epsilon-greedy action selection (only in training mode)\n   143\t        if self.training and self.rng.random() < self.epsilon:\n   144\t            idx = int(self.rng.randint(self.n_actions))\n   145\t        else:\n   146\t            with torch.no_grad():\n   147\t                q = self.net(x.unsqueeze(0)).squeeze(0).cpu().numpy()\n   148\t            idx = int(np.argmax(q))\n   149\t\n   150\t        if self.training:\n   151\t            # Store transition placeholder; reward will be set later via store_reward()\n   152\t            self._last_obs = observation\n   153\t            self._last_action_idx = idx\n   154\t            self._last_obs_tensor = x\n   155\t\n   156\t        return dict(self.actions[idx])\n   157\t\n   158\t    def store_reward(self, reward: float, next_observation: Dict[str, Any], done: bool):\n   159\t        \"\"\"Store a complete transition (obs, action, reward, next_obs, done) in the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n   160\t        # Always track total reward (for both train and test)\n   161\t        self.total_reward += reward\n   162\t        self.current_sweep_reward += reward\n   163\t        \n   164\t        if not self.training:\n   165\t            return\n   166\t\n   167\t        next_obs_tensor = self._obs_to_tensor(next_observation)\n   168\t        transition = (\n   169\t            self._last_obs_tensor.clone(),\n   170\t            self._last_action_idx,\n   171\t            reward,\n   172\t            next_obs_tensor.clone(),\n   173\t            done\n   174\t        )\n   175\t\n   176\t        if len(self.replay_buffer) < self.buffer_capacity:\n   177\t            self.replay_buffer.append(transition)\n   178\t        else:\n   179\t            self.replay_buffer[self.buffer_pos] = transition\n   180\t            self.buffer_pos = (self.buffer_pos + 1) % self.buffer_capacity\n   181\t\n   182\t        self.step_count += 1\n   183\t\n   184\t        # Record return every N updates and reset counter\n   185\t        if self.step_count % self.return_record_interval == 0:\n   186\t            self.reward_history.append(self.current_sweep_reward)\n   187\t            self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   188\t\n   189\t        # Train every update_freq steps\n   190\t        if self.step_count % self.update_freq == 0 and len(self.replay_buffer) >= self.batch_size:\n   191\t            loss = self._train_step()\n   192\t            self.total_loss += loss\n   193\t            self.loss_history.append(loss)\n   194\t\n   195\t    def _train_step(self) -> float:\n   196\t        \"\"\"Perform one DQN training step on a batch from the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n   197\t        batch = self.rng.choice(len(self.replay_buffer), self.batch_size, replace=False).tolist()\n   198\t        batch = [self.replay_buffer[i] for i in batch]\n   199\t\n   200\t        obs_b = torch.stack([t[0] for t in batch])\n   201\t        act_b = torch.tensor([t[1] for t in batch], dtype=torch.long, device=self.device)\n   202\t        rew_b = torch.tensor([t[2] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   203\t        next_b = torch.stack([t[3] for t in batch])\n   204\t        done_b = torch.tensor([t[4] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   205\t\n   206\t        q_values = self.net(obs_b)\n   207\t        q_sa = q_values.gather(1, act_b.unsqueeze(1)).squeeze(1)\n   208\t\n   209\t        with torch.no_grad():\n   210\t            q_next = self.target_net(next_b)\n   211\t            q_next_max = q_next.max(1)[0]\n   212\t            target = rew_b + self.gamma * (1.0 - done_b) * q_next_max\n   213\t\n   214\t        loss = F.mse_loss(q_sa, target)\n   215\t        self.optimizer.zero_grad()\n   216\t        loss.backward()\n   217\t        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(self.net.parameters(), self.max_grad_norm)\n   218\t        self.optimizer.step()\n   219\t\n   220\t        # Update target network periodically\n   221\t        if self.step_count % self.target_update_freq == 0:\n   222\t            self.target_net.load_state_dict(self.net.state_dict())\n   223\t            # Decay epsilon\n   224\t            self.epsilon = max(self.epsilon_end, self.epsilon * self.epsilon_decay)\n   225\t\n   226\t        return loss.item()\n   227\t\n   228\t    def reset_loss(self):\n   229\t        \"\"\"Reset loss and reward counters for a new sweep.\"\"\"\n   230\t        self.total_loss = 0.0\n   231\t        self.total_reward = 0.0\n   232\t        self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   233\t        self.reward_history = []\n   234\t\n   235\t    def get_loss_history(self) -> list:\n   236\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded losses.\"\"\"\n   237\t        return self.loss_history\n   238\t\n   239\t    def get_reward_history(self) -> list:\n   240\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded returns (every N updates).\"\"\"\n   241\t        return self.reward_history\n   242\t\n   243\t    def get_total_loss(self) -> float:\n   244\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative training loss.\"\"\"\n   245\t        return self.total_loss\n   246\t\n   247\t    def get_total_reward(self) -> float:\n   248\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative reward from environment interactions.\"\"\"\n   249\t        return self.total_reward\n   250\t\n   251\t    def action_to_index(self, action: Dict[str, float]) -> int:\n   252\t        a = np.array([action.get('F', 0.8), action.get('CR', 0.9)], dtype=float)\n   253\t        acts = np.array([[aa['F'], aa['CR']] for aa in self.actions], dtype=float)\n   254\t        d = np.sum((acts - a[None, :])**2, axis=1)\n   255\t        return int(np.argmin(d))\n   256\t\n   257\t    def save(self, path: str):\n   258\t        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n   259\t\n   260\t    def load(self, path: str):\n   261\t        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n\"\n    }\n    </json>"

`step`\n    returns the greedy action as a dict (matching ParamController).\n\n    Online training: stores transitions in a replay buffer and performs\n    a DQN update every `update_freq` steps.\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, model_path: str = None, obs_dim: int = 3, bins: int = 5,\n                device: str = 'cpu', training: bool = False, update_freq: int = 10,\n                buffer_capacity: int = 1000, batch_size: int = 64,\n                lr: float = 1e-4, gamma: float = 0.99, target_update_freq: int = 50,\n                max_grad_norm: float = 1.0, epsilon_start: float = 1.0,\n                epsilon_end: float = 0.05, epsilon_decay: float = 0.999,\n                seed: int = None):\n        if torch is None:\n            raise RuntimeError('torch is required for DiscreteDQNController')\n        self.device = torch.device(device)\n        self.obs_dim = obs_dim\n        self.bins = int(bins)\n        self.seed = seed if seed is not None else 42\n        \n        # \u8bbe\u7f6ePyTorch\u79cd\u5b50\u786e\u4fdd\u7f51\u7edc\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u53ef\u590d\u73b0\n        torch.manual_seed(self.seed)\n        if torch.cuda.is_available():\n            torch.cuda.manual_seed(self.seed)\n            torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(self.seed)\n        \n        # \u786e\u4fddPyTorch\u64cd\u4f5c\u5b8c\u5168\u786e\u5b9a\u6027\n        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True\n        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False\n        torch.backends.cudnn.enabled = False  # \u7981\u7528cudnn\u4ee5\u5b8c\u5168\u6d88\u9664\u968f\u673a\u6027\n        \n        self.rng = np.random.RandomState(self.seed)\n\n        # DE parameter discretization\n        self.F_vals = np.linspace(0.1, 1.0, self.bins)\n        self.CR_vals = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, self.bins)\n\n        self.actions = []\"\n    }\n    </json>"

Do NOT just describe changes - you must USE the editor tool with command='str_replace' to make the changes.\n    38\t\n    39\t## Codebase Structure (/hada/metabbo/):\n    40\t\n    41\tThe codebase has two main layers:\n    42\t\n    43\t### 1. Evolutionary Algorithm Layer (ec_algorithm.py)\n    44\t- **ec_algorithm.py**: The main evolutionary algorithm implementation. Contains the optimizer class that handles population initialization, iteration loop, solution evaluation, and result tracking. This is where you implement the core search algorithm (PSO, DE, CMA-ES, etc.).\n    45\t- **param_controller.py**: Parameter controller interface. Defines the abstract interface for dynamic parameter adjustment. You can implement custom controllers here.\n    46\t\n    47\t### 2. Meta-Learning Layer (meta_learning.py, meta_learning_env.py, train_meta_learning.py)\n    48\t- **meta_learning.py**: Meta-learning controller that dynamically adjusts algorithm parameters during optimization. Uses a neural network to map observations to parameter values. Supports both continuous and discrete action spaces.\n    49\t- **meta_learning_env.py**: Environment wrapper for training the meta-learning controller. Provides a gym-like interface with reset/step methods.\n    50\t- **train_meta_learning.py**: Training scripts for the meta-learning controller. Contains replay buffer and training logic.\n    51\t\n    52\t## CRITICAL: Try Different Evolutionary Algorithms!\n    53\t\n    54\tPrevious generations have mostly made small PSO parameter tweaks (adjusting w, c1, c2, adding turbulence, changing initialization). To achieve significant improvements, you should try fundamentally different approaches.\n    55\t\n    56\t**Consider replacing or substantially modifying the algorithm:**\n    57\t- **DE (Differential Evolution)**: Excellent for continuous optimization, strong exploration\n    58\t- **CMA-ES**: State-of-the-art for black-box optimization, adapts covariance matrix\n    59\t- **SHADE/L-SHADE**: Success-History based Adaptive DE, CEC competition winner\n    60\t- **JADE**: Adaptive DE with optional external archive\n    61\t- **ES (Evolution Strategies)**: Simple but effective, uses mutation + selection\n    62\t- Or any other evolutionary algorithm you think is suitable\n    63\t\n    64\t**You can also try:**\n    65\t- Hybrid algorithms combining multiple approaches\n    66\t- Multi-population / island model with migration\n    67\t- Archive-based selection mechanisms\n    68\t- Different initialization strategies, boundary handling, diversity maintenance\n    69\t\n    70\t**Meta-Learning Layer:**\n    71\t- Try different neural network architectures (deeper, wider, different activations)\n    72\t- Change the observation space (what information the controller sees)\n    73\t- Change the action space (what parameters the controller outputs)\n    74\t- Try different training strategies (reward design, learning rate, exploration)\n Contains replay buffer and training logic.\n    51\t\n    52\t## CRITICAL: Try Different Evolutionary Algorithms!\n    53\t\n    54\tPrevious generations have mostly made small PSO parameter tweaks (adjusting w, c1, c2, adding turbulence, changing initialization). **SHADE/L-SHADE**: Success-History based Adaptive DE, CEC competition winner\n    60\t- **JADE**: Adaptive DE with optional external archive\n    61\t- **ES (Evolution Strategies)**: Simple but effective, uses mutation + selection\n    62\t- Or any other evolutionary algorithm you think is suitable\n    63\t\n    64\t**You can also try:**\n    65\t- Hybrid algorithms combining multiple Main loop\n        iteration = 0\n        done = False\n        \n        while not done:\n            iteration += 1\n            params = dict(self.params)\n            if self.controller is not None:\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\n                    params.update(step_params)\n            \n            F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\n            CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\n            \n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n            \n            # Generate trial vectors\n            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                # Tournament selection (tournsize=3, does NOT exclude current individual)\n                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\n                if len(candidates) < 3:\n                    indices = list(range(pop_size))\n                else:\n                    indices = rng.choice(candidates, size=3, replace=False)\n                \n                a, b, c = pop[indices[0]], pop[indices[1]], pop[indices[2]]\n                \n                # Mutation and crossover\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\n                for j in range(self.dim):\n                    if rng.random() < CR or j == j_rand:\n                        new_pop[i, j] = a[j] + F * (b[j] - c[j])\n                \n                # selected[0]\n    75\t        for idx in selected[1:]:\n    76\t            if fitness[idx] < fitness[best_idx]:\n    77\t                best_idx = idx\n    78\t        return best_idx\n    79\t\n    80\t    def _sample_cauchy(self, rng, loc, scale):\n    81\t        \"\"\"Sample one value from a truncated Cauchy-like distribution.\"\"\"\n    82\t        return loc + scale * np.tan(np.pi * (rng.random() - 0.5))\n    83\t\n    84\t    def _sample_normal(self, rng, loc, scale):\n    85\t        \"\"\"Sample one value from a normal distribution.\"\"\"\n    86\t        return rng.normal(loc, scale)\n    87\t\n    88\t    def _reflect(self, x, lb, ub):\n    89\t        \"\"\"Reflect out-of-bound coordinates back into the feasible box.\"\"\"\n    90\t        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)\n    91\t        lb = np.asarray(lb, dtype=float)\n    92\t        ub = np.asarray(ub, dtype=float)\n    93\t        span = ub - lb\n    94\t        # Reflect repeatedly to handle very far values\n    95\t        for _ in range(5):\n    96\t            below = x < lb\n    97\t            above = x > ub\n    98\t            if not (np.any(below) or np.any(above)):\n    99\t                break\n   100\t            x[below] = lb[below] + (lb[below] - x[below])\n   101\t            x[above] = ub[above] - (x[above] - ub[above])\n   102\t        # Final safety clip\n   103\t        return np.clip(x, lb, ub)\n   104\t\n   105\t    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\n   106\t        if self.controller is not None:\n   107\t            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\n   108\t\n   109\t        pop_size = self.pop_size\n   110\t        lb = self.lower_bounds\n   111\t        ub = self.upper_bounds\n   112\t        \n   113\t        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\n   114\t        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\n   115\t\n   116\t        # Initialize population - uniform random initialization\n   117\t        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\n   118\t        \n   119\t        # Evaluate initial population\n   120\t        evals = 0\n   121\t        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\n   122\t        \n   123\t        for i in range(pop_size):\n   124\t            if evals >= self.max_evals:\n   125\t                break\n   126\t            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\n   127\t            evals += 1\n   128\t\n   129\t        # Track best\n   130\t        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\n   131\t        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\n   132\t        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\n   133\t        \n   134\t        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\n   135\t        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\n   136\t        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\n   137\t\n   138\t        # JADE archive: stores replaced individuals to provide extra diversity\n   139\t        archive = []\n   140\t\n   141\t        # Main loop\n   142\t        iteration = 0\n   143\t        done = False\n   144\t        \n   145\t        while not done:\n   146\t            iteration += 1\n   147\t            params = dict(self.params)\n   148\t            if self.controller is not None:\n   149\t                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n   150\t                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n   151\t                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\n   152\t                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\n   153\t                    params.update(step_params)\n   154\t            \n   155\t            F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\n   156\t            CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\n   157\t            strategy = str(params.get('strategy', 'rand/1'))\n   158\t            \n   159\t            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n   160\t            \n   161\t            # Generate trial vectors\n   162\t            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\n   163\t            for i in range(pop_size):\n   164\t                # JADE/current-to-pbest style base: use current individual as base\n   165\t                if strategy in ('current-to-pbest', 'jade') and pop_size >= 2:\n   166\t                    # Select pbest from top portion of population (p=20%)\n   167\t                    p = max(1, int(0.2 * pop_size))\n   168\t                    top_indices = np.argsort(fitness)[:p]\n   169\t                    pbest_idx = top_indices[rng.randint(0, p)]\n   170\t                    \n   171\t                    # Select two distinct random individuals, different from i and pbest\n   172\t                    candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i and idx != pbest_idx]\n   173\t                    if len(candidates) >= 2:\n   174\t                        r1, r2 = rng.choice(candidates, size=2, replace=False)\n   175\t                        x_pbest = pop[pbest_idx]\n   176\t                        x_r1 = pop[int(r1)]\n   177\t                        x_r2 = pop[int(r2)]\n   178\t                        \n   179\t                        # Use archive member for second difference vector (JADE)\n   180\t                        if archive and rng.random() < 0.5:\n   181\t                            arch_idx = int(rng.randint(0, len(archive)))\n   182\t                            x_arch = archive[arch_idx]\n   183\t                            mutant = new_pop[i] + F * (x_pbest - new_pop[i]) + F * (x_r1 - x_arch)\n   184\t                        else:\n   185\t                            mutant = new_pop[i] + F * (x_pbest - new_pop[i]) + F * (x_r1 - x_r2)\n   186\t                    else:\n   187\t                        # Fallback to rand/1 if not enough candidates\n   188\t                        candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\n   189\t                        a_idx, b_idx, c_idx = rng.choice(candidates, size=3, replace=False)\n   190\t                        mutant = pop[a_idx] + F * (pop[b_idx] - pop[c_idx])\n   191\t                else:\n   192\t                    # Standard rand/1 mutation\n   193\t                    candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\n   194\t                    if len(candidates) < 3:\n   195\t                        a_idx, b_idx, c_idx = rng.choice(pop_size, size=3, replace=False)\n   196\t                    else:\n   197\t                        a_idx, b_idx, c_idx = rng.choice(candidates, size=3, replace=False)\n   198\t                    \n   199\t                    a, b, c = pop[a_idx], pop[b_idx], pop[c_idx]\n   200\t                    \n   201\t                    # Use Cauchy-distributed F for better exploration (JADE-style)\n   202\t                    local_F = F\n   203\t                    if rng.random() < 0.5:\n   204\t                        # Occasionally sample from lighter/heavier tail\n   205\t                        local_F = float(np.clip(self._sample_cauchy(rng, F, 0.1), 0.0, 1.5))\n   206\t                    mutant = a + local_F * (b - c)\n   207\t                \n   208\t                # Binomial crossover\n   209\t                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\n   210\t                trial = np.copy(new_pop[i])\n   211\t                for j in range(self.dim):\n   212\t                    if rng.random() < CR or j == j_rand:\n   213\t                        trial[j] = mutant[j]\n   214\t                \n   215\t                # Boundary handling: reflect instead of simply clipping\n   216\t                new_pop[i] = self._reflect(trial, lb, ub)\n   217\t            \n   218\t            # Evaluate trial vectors\n   219\t            for i in range(pop_size):\n   220\t                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n   221\t                    done = True\n   222\t                    break\n   223\t                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\n   224\t                evals += 1\n   225\t                \n   226\t                # Greedy selection with JADE archive handling\n   227\t                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\n   228\t                    # Add the replaced individual to the archive (keep bounded)\n   229\t                    archive.append(pop[i].copy())\n   230\t                    if len(archive) > pop_size:\n   231\t                        # Randomly remove one archive member to bound memory\n   232\t                        archive.pop(int(rng.randint(0, len(archive))))\n   233\t                    \n   234\t                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\n   235\t                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n   236\t                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n   237\t                        gbest_f = trial_f\n   238\t                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\n   239\t            \n   240\t            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\n   241\t            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\n   242\t            \n   243\t            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\n   244\t            if self.controller is not None and train:\n   245\t                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n   246\t                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n   247\t                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n   248\t                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n   249\t                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\n   250\t            \n   251\t            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n   252\t\n   253\t        self.stats = {\n   254\t            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\n   255\t            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\n   256\t            'evals': int(evals),\n   257\t            'iterations': int(iteration),\n   258\t            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n   259\t            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\n   260\t            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\n   261\t        }\n   262\t        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\"\n    }\n    </json>",

If `path` is a directory, `view` lists non-hidden files and directories up to 2 levels deep\\n* The `create` command cannot be used if the specified `path` already exists as a file\\n* If a `command` generates a long output, it will be truncated and marked with `<response clipped>`\\n* The `undo_edit` command will revert the last edit made to the file at `path`\\n\\nNotes for using the `str_replace` command:\\n* The `old_str` parameter should match EXACTLY one or more consecutive lines from the original file. 9\tfrom typing import Optional, Dict, Any, Tuple\n    10\timport numpy as np\n    11\timport os\n    12\timport sys\n    13\t\n    14\t# Import SEED from config if available\n    15\t_config_seed = None\n    16\tsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\n    17\tfrom config import SEED as _config_seed\n    18\t\n    19\t\n    20\tdef evaluate_problem(problem, x):\n    21\t    \"\"\"Evaluate problem, handling both MetaBox (problem.func) and cocoex (problem(x)) styles.\"\"\"\n    22\t    if not isinstance(x, np.ndarray):\n    23\t        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)\n    24\t    \n    25\t    if hasattr(problem, 'func'):\n    26\t        # MetaBox style: use problem.func(x), which REQUIRES 2D input [NP, dim]\n    27\t        if x.ndim == 1:\n    28\t            x = x.reshape(1, -1)\n    29\t        result = problem.func(x)\n    30\t        return float(np.mean(np.array(result).flatten()))\n    31\t    else:\n    32\t        # cocoex style: problem(x)\n    33\t        return float(problem(x))\n    34\t\n    35\t\n    36\tclass DEOptimizer:\n    37\t    \"\"\"Differential Evolution optimizer with DQN controller support.\n    38\t    \n    39\t    The controller outputs F and CR parameters dynamically during optimization.\n    40\t    \"\"\"\n    41\t    def __init__(self, dim: int, lower_bounds, upper_bounds, max_evals: 45\t        if lower_bounds is not None:\n    46\t            self.lower_bounds = np.asarray(lower_bounds, dtype=float)\n    47\t            if self.lower_bounds.ndim == 0:\n    48\t                self.lower_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.lower_bounds))\n    49\t        else:\n    50\t            self.lower_bounds = -5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n    51\t        \n    52\t        if upper_bounds is not None:\n    53\t            self.upper_bounds = np.asarray(upper_bounds, dtype=float)\n    54\t            if self.upper_bounds.ndim == 0:\n    55\t                self.upper_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.upper_bounds))\n    56\t        else:\n    57\t            self.upper_bounds = 5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n    58\t        \n    59\t        self.max_evals = int(max_evals)\n    60\t        self.params = dict(params) if params is not None else {}\n    61\t        self.controller = controller\n    62\t        self.recorder = recorder\n    63\t        self.stats = {}\n    64\t        self.pop_size = swarm_size if swarm_size is not None else max(10, 5 * self.dim)\n    65\t        self.F = float(self.params.get('F', 0.5))\n    66\t        self.CR = float(self.params.get('CR', 0.5))\n    67\t\n    68\t    def _tournament_select(self, fitness, tournsize, rng):\n    69\t        \"\"\"Tournament selection with tournsize.\"\"\"\n    70\t selected[0]\n    75\t        for idx in selected[1:]:\n    76\t            if fitness[idx] < fitness[best_idx]:\n    77\t                best_idx = idx\n    78\t        return best_idx\n    79\t\n    80\t    def _sample_cauchy(self, rng, loc, scale):\n    81\t        \"\"\"Sample one value from a truncated Cauchy-like distribution.\"\"\"\n    82\t        return loc + scale * np.tan(np.pi * np.asarray(x, dtype=float)\n    91\t        lb = np.asarray(lb, dtype=float)\n    92\t        ub = np.asarray(ub, dtype=float)\n    93\t        span = ub - lb\n    94\t        # Reflect repeatedly to
